# Tasks and records, claim by claim

Generated by `tools/claims.py tables`; do not edit. For every claim of [CLAIMS.md](../CLAIMS.md) that has tasks: each task with its predicted value, the stored result that carries the original number (a file of the data archive, [DATA.md](../DATA.md)), and the latest record of the task under the scheduler, if there is one. A task without a record reads "not re-run": the stored result is then the only support, and it is the original output, not a run of this repository.

A predicted value is a closed form of the article, a number the article prints, or a count copied from a stored result ([expected.json](expected.json) says which). "Computed" is what the task found; the status is `ok` when the two agree. A record names the day it was computed and the commit of the code.

<a name="claim-thm-5-1"></a>
